General Malaysia fallback questions are not included because they do not belong to these destinations. Wording, answers and hints are reproduced as stored; this is not a factual verification. Missing hints are marked explicitly.

## KL Bird Park

### Active questions (20)

#### 1. Opened in 1992 near the bird park, this neighboring attraction features over 5,000 winged insects flying among exotic plants and ferns.

Category: Guess the Word  
Question ID: 065f8b75-1519-45db-84de-5587085abded


**Correct answer:** BUTTERFLY

- **Hint 1:** It is a nectar-feeding insect with two pairs of large, typically brightly colored wings.
- **Hint 2:** It is named after a dairy product and a flying insect.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is BUTTERFLY.

#### 2. The KL Bird Park is famous for being billed as the world's largest park of this specific type, meaning it has a giant canopy or net overhead.

Category: Guess the Word  
Question ID: 1dab4fea-1178-4506-92b6-8a049a411072


**Correct answer:** COVERED

- **Hint 1:** It means having a roof or protective canopy over it.

## Sultan Abdul Samad Jamek Mosque

### Active questions (20)

#### 1. What architectural style is used for the design of the mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 06bb5481-c42e-4b5a-9274-7f09c1707e5e

- A: Indo-Saracenic style
- B: Gothic Revival style
- C: Brutalist architecture
- D: Neoclassical style

**Correct answer:** Indo-Saracenic style

- **Hint 1:** It loosely reflects Indian Muslim Mughal architectural style.
- **Hint 2:** It is also described as Moorish architecture.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Indo-Saracenic style.

#### 2. What was the height of the largest dome of the mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 16e7569c-e1df-404f-959f-86049a820677

- A: 21.3 metres
- B: 15.5 metres
- C: 30.2 metres
- D: 45.0 metres

**Correct answer:** 21.3 metres

- **Hint 1:** It is equivalent to about 70 feet.
- **Hint 2:** The mosque features a total of 3 domes.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is 21.3 metres.

#### 3. Which metro transit lines serve the busy interchange station located just outside the mosque compound?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 25131258-4470-41b4-81a1-a85638e2a7eb

- A: Kelana Jaya Line, Sri Petaling Line, and Ampang Line
- B: Kajang Line, Putrajaya Line, and Monorail Line
- C: Port Klang Line, Seremban Line, and LRT Line 1
- D: KLIA Express, KLIA Transit, and LRT Line 2

**Correct answer:** Kelana Jaya Line, Sri Petaling Line, and Ampang Line

- **Hint 1:** It shares its name with the mosque itself.
- **Hint 2:** The station acts as a major metro interchange between multiple lines.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Kelana Jaya Line, Sri Petaling Line, and Ampang Line.

#### 4. Who was the fourth Sultan of Selangor and the ancestor after whom the mosque was renamed in 2017?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 343f46b6-0f4b-44d9-9ff7-1243128d550b

- A: Sultan Abdul Samad
- B: Sultan Sir Alaeddin Sulaiman Shah
- C: Sultan Sharafuddin Idris Shah
- D: Sultan Salahuddin Abdul Aziz Shah

**Correct answer:** Sultan Abdul Samad

- **Hint 1:** His name appears directly in the current official title of the mosque.
- **Hint 2:** The land on which the mosque was originally built was part of his state.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Sultan Abdul Samad.

#### 5. What notable modification was made to the mosque's original open-air structure during later expansions?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 36afd4d6-cb78-4e2c-b529-9ee6201e78a6

- A: The addition of a roof over the forecourt
- B: The enclosure of the main prayer hall with glass walls
- C: The conversion of the courtyard into a reflection pool
- D: The removal of smaller domes to add a second floor

**Correct answer:** The addition of a roof over the forecourt

- **Hint 1:** Initially, a certain part of the complex was completely exposed to the sky.
- **Hint 2:** Extensions were built to shelter visitors from the elements.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is The addition of a roof over the forecourt.

#### 6. When was the mosque officially renamed Sultan Abdul Samad Jamek Mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 4d125e4c-fc5f-44fd-8650-f2d0c4bd22d9

- A: 23 June 2017
- B: 15 August 2005
- C: 31 December 1999
- D: 10 January 1984

**Correct answer:** 23 June 2017

- **Hint 1:** It was renamed by Selangor's Sultan Sharafuddin Idris Shah.
- **Hint 2:** It happened during the month of June in the late 2010s.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is 23 June 2017.

#### 7. Locally, what alternative name is commonly used by residents to refer to the mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 56a14cd9-e0c7-4031-b785-491473747ed4

- A: Friday Mosque
- B: River Mosque
- C: Confluence Mosque
- D: Heritage Mosque

**Correct answer:** Friday Mosque

- **Hint 1:** The name relates to the holy day of congregation in Islam.
- **Hint 2:** It reflects the local vernacular usage alongside the formal name.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Friday Mosque.

#### 8. What is the meaning of the word "Jamek" in Arabic, from which the mosque's name is derived?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 589e4a5d-2b9a-4663-b0bf-7f90566d764e

- A: A place where people congregate to worship
- B: A quiet sanctuary for solitary prayer
- C: A grand tower of light
- D: A riverbank garden

**Correct answer:** A place where people congregate to worship

- **Hint 1:** It comes from the Arabic word jāmiʿ.
- **Hint 2:** It relates to people gathering together.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is A place where people congregate to worship.

#### 9. Which specific minaret was underpinned during the 1984 refurbishment because it was sloping?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 5a6ceb3a-05fe-4a8e-8bf2-004063ead0f5

- A: The minaret nearest the river
- B: The tallest minaret near the main entrance
- C: The northern corner minaret
- D: The minaret closest to Jalan Tun Perak

**Correct answer:** The minaret nearest the river

- **Hint 1:** The geographical feature flowing right past the mosque influenced its stability.
- **Hint 2:** It required structural underpinning during the 1984 work.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is The minaret nearest the river.

#### 10. Which of the following areas is located nearby the mosque alongside Chinatown and Little India?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 64c1af4f-9852-4afc-ace6-0c5cb81f131a

- A: Dataran Merdeka
- B: KLCC Park
- C: Bukit Bintang
- D: Kampung Baru

**Correct answer:** Dataran Merdeka

- **Hint 1:** It is a famous historic square situated close to the transit station and mosque.
- **Hint 2:** The area is well-known for its historical significance and landmark flagpole.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Dataran Merdeka.

#### 11. What incident occurred in 1993 affecting one of the structural domes of the mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 6e300d26-cace-453a-9c04-6290978d3d71

- A: It collapsed due to heavy rain
- B: It was struck by lightning and caught fire
- C: It was intentionally dismantled for expansion
- D: It suffered cracks from a minor earthquake

**Correct answer:** It collapsed due to heavy rain

- **Hint 1:** Weather conditions played a major role in this event.
- **Hint 2:** The damaged part has since been repaired.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is It collapsed due to heavy rain.

#### 12. In what year was the Sultan Abdul Samad Jamek Mosque officially opened?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 7dcce7a3-71b0-41c3-a3a1-3684fd734488

- A: 1905
- B: 1909
- C: 1912
- D: 1965

**Correct answer:** 1909

- **Hint 1:** It opened in December of that year.
- **Hint 2:** The foundation stone was laid the previous year in 1908.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is 1909.

#### 13. Near the confluence of which two rivers is the mosque located?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 94c6b23d-71f3-4ae6-af56-85e3a1e18d94

- A: Klang and Gombak
- B: Pahang and Muar
- C: Perak and Selangor
- D: Melaka and Linggi

**Correct answer:** Klang and Gombak

- **Hint 1:** They meet right at an old Malay burial place.
- **Hint 2:** Both river names start with K and G.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Klang and Gombak.

#### 14. What type of location did the mosque occupy prior to its construction?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 9e6915c0-b185-446b-a8b5-e75d2cdb8718

- A: An old Malay burial place
- B: A former British military outpost
- C: A traditional local marketplace
- D: An abandoned rubber plantation

**Correct answer:** An old Malay burial place

- **Hint 1:** It was situated right at the confluence of the Klang and Gombak rivers.
- **Hint 2:** The site had religious or commemorative significance before the structure was erected.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is An old Malay burial place.

#### 15. What was the total cost of construction for the mosque when it was built?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: ab4caf5e-1ffc-45e5-b59a-0da3a082a804

- A: $32,625
- B: $50,000
- C: $15,400
- D: $100,200

**Correct answer:** $32,625

- **Hint 1:** It is an exact figure in the tens of thousands.
- **Hint 2:** The construction was funded in part by the Malay community and the British colonial government.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is $32,625.

#### 16. What nickname has been given to the pink and white banding pattern of the minarets formed of brick and plaster?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: bf2b4dfd-5ba4-4165-a0c4-32d81026e408

- A: Blood and bandage
- B: Brick and bone
- C: Rose and ribbon
- D: Clay and crust

**Correct answer:** Blood and bandage

- **Hint 1:** The description uses the word 'blood'.
- **Hint 2:** The second part of the nickname sounds like a medical wrap.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Blood and bandage.

#### 17. Which Sultan laid the foundation stone of the mosque on 23 March 1908?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: ca0ded76-32dc-4420-b334-f75e654c9095

- A: Sultan Sir Alaeddin Sulaiman Shah
- B: Sultan Sharafuddin Idris Shah
- C: Sultan Abdul Samad
- D: Sultan Iskandar Shah

**Correct answer:** Sultan Sir Alaeddin Sulaiman Shah

- **Hint 1:** He was the Sultan of Selangor at the time.
- **Hint 2:** His name includes Alaeddin Sulaiman.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Sultan Sir Alaeddin Sulaiman Shah.

#### 18. Which architect designed the Sultan Abdul Samad Jamek Mosque?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: ddcd6674-e76f-46f8-b131-eeaaddd2b274

- C: Reginald Arthur
- D: Charles Benison

- **Hint 2:** His initials are A. B.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Arthur Benison Hubback.

## Petaling Street Market

### Active questions (57)

#### 1. During which conflict did the tin mines in the area become abandoned and flooded?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 0152191e-3788-4acf-871e-5020b5dbc29a

- A: Selangor Civil War
- B: World War II
- C: Malayan Emergency
- D: Opium Wars

**Correct answer:** Selangor Civil War

- **Hint 1:** It occurred in the 1860s to 1870s.
- **Hint 2:** It involved rival Malay factions and Chinese secret societies.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Selangor Civil War.

#### 2. What is the literal English translation of the name Kwai Chai Hong?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 07016065-444a-42d4-a42e-ffe0c1df301b

- A: Little Ghost Lane or Little Demon Lane
- B: Golden Dragon Alley
- C: Starch Factory Street
- D: Pioneer Settlement Road

**Correct answer:** Little Ghost Lane or Little Demon Lane

- **Hint 1:** Refers to a back alley behind shophouses along Lorong Panggung.
- **Hint 2:** Contains mural arts depicting historic local life.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Little Ghost Lane or Little Demon Lane.

#### 3. What name is given to the green roof cover constructed over Petaling Street during its 2003 renovation?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 0aee7381-07a5-454d-82a8-16e1a860f6a6

- A: Green Dragon
- B: Jade Serpent
- C: Emerald Canopy
- D: Forest Roof

**Correct answer:** Green Dragon

- **Hint 1:** It covers the entire pedestrianised road.
- **Hint 2:** Part of the RM11 million facelift.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Green Dragon.

#### 4. What architectural style is associated with the old Jamek Mosque located within walking distance of the area?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 0f8d06cf-79c5-4fb0-b2c6-cc68771da395

- A: Moorish architecture
- B: Brutalist architecture
- C: Neo-Gothic architecture
- D: Sino-Portuguese architecture

**Correct answer:** Moorish architecture

- **Hint 1:** It describes the historical design style of the mosque.

## The National Museum of Malaysia

### Active questions (10)

#### 1. What gift from the government of Pakistan is used to decorate the floor of the central section of the main museum building?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 4821faba-4236-462e-9a1b-2267b05f55b1

- B: Marble pillars
- C: Carved wooden panels
- D: Bronze statues

**Correct answer:** Special tiles

- **Hint 1:** These items feature blue geometric designs.
- **Hint 2:** They are laid out on the floor of the Central Hall.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Special tiles.

#### 2. On what date was the National Museum in Kuala Lumpur officially inaugurated?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 4861cbb4-aaef-4540-89a5-e1d45717100f

- A: 31 August 1957
- B: 31 August 1963
- C: 10 March 1945
- D: 4 April 1996

**Correct answer:** 31 August 1963

- **Hint 1:** It opened on the same day and month as Malaysia's independence, but a few years later.
- **Hint 2:** The year was 1963.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is 31 August 1963.

#### 3. Who was the architect that designed the new National Museum building, drawing inspiration from Malay royal palaces and vernacular architecture?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 5aaa8e4a-09b9-47eb-a750-e85e3698feda

- A: Arthur Benison Hubback
- B: Ho Kok Hoe
- C: Leonard Wray Jr.
- D: Mubin Sheppard

**Correct answer:** Ho Kok Hoe

- **Hint 1:** His surname starts with an H.
- **Hint 2:** He was inspired by Malay royal palaces.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Ho Kok Hoe.

#### 4. Which act was used to gazette the museum building as an ancient monument and historical site on 4 April 1996?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 5b5848b0-41e7-420a-921d-9f38e288e5e6

- A: Antiquities Act 169/1976
- B: National Heritage Act 2005
- C: Federated Malay States Act
- D: Museum Conservation Decree

**Correct answer:** Antiquities Act 169/1976

- **Hint 1:** The legislation includes the number 169.
- **Hint 2:** It is referred to as the Antiquities Act.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Antiquities Act 169/1976.

#### 5. Which vintage vehicle on outdoor display in the museum's vicinity is the first national car, launched on July 9, 1985?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: 7d63141c-a555-489d-9138-63989109b03e

- A: Melaka Bullock Cart
- B: Kitson Steam Locomotive
- C: Proton Saga
- D: Tin Dredge

- **Hint 1:** It is a 1.3-liter motorized vehicle.
- **Hint 2:** The model name is Saga.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Proton Saga.

#### 6. Which original-size old Terengganu timber palace, built from cengal wood, was erected in the museum compound in April 1974?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: a48f4a75-b3c6-4932-be90-5606f01a7d1e

- A: Istana Maziah
- B: Istana Satu
- C: Kota Istana
- D: Istana Negara

**Correct answer:** Istana Satu

- **Hint 1:** It is built in the traditional form known as 'Rumah Tiang Dua Belas'.
- **Hint 2:** It was originally erected by Sultan Zainal Abidin III.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Istana Satu.

#### 7. Who was the first director of the National Museum serving from 1959 to 1963?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: a976540d-f13d-456b-b9b7-f1a5053b4314

- B: Mubin Sheppard
- C: J.J. Lowry
- D: Zulkifli bin Abd. Aziz

**Correct answer:** Mubin Sheppard

- **Hint 1:** His tenure began during the construction phase before official inauguration.
- **Hint 2:** His last name sounds like a person who tends sheep.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Mubin Sheppard.

#### 8. What are located next to the Istana Satu building in the museum compound?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: b441fdf7-35c8-4735-bb8a-19c7463a343b

- A: Two keliriengs or burial poles
- B: Two bronze busts of royalty
- C: Two vintage steam locomotives
- D: Two mosaic murals from Pakistan

**Correct answer:** Two keliriengs or burial poles

- **Hint 1:** They are carved from huge hardwood tree trunks.
- **Hint 2:** They were historically used to hold jars containing a chief's bones.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Two keliriengs or burial poles.

#### 9. What type of outdoor rail transport exhibit on display at the museum was put into service in 1921 and covered 1.5 million rail miles before ceasing operation in 1969?

Category: Multiple Choice Question

- A: An early electric tram
- B: A steam locomotive made by Kitson & Co
- C: A Melaka bullock cart
- D: A tin dredge factory

- **Hint 1:** It was manufactured in England.
- **Hint 2:** It is a railway engine powered by steam.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is A steam locomotive made by Kitson & Co.

#### 10. Which historical institution served as the de facto national museum before independence and was later completely demolished to make way for the new National Museum?

Category: Multiple Choice Question  
Question ID: e4f76854-c914-466d-a6b6-5e8524511a90

- A: Perak Museum
- B: Selangor Museum
- C: Raffles Museum
- D: Singapore Museum

**Correct answer:** Selangor Museum

- **Hint 1:** It was established in 1887 as an amateur affair.
- **Hint 2:** Its right wing was bombed by US B-29 bombers in 1945.
- **Hint 3:** The correct answer is Selangor Museum.
